codex
The useful parallel structure is one GPU thread block per possible seed, with threads cooperatively evaluating candidate points. Each block preserves the original greedy insertion order and index-based tie-breaking, while all seed clusters are built concurrently. I’m implementing that structure with incremental diameter updates so distances are not recomputed against the full cluster on every iteration.

+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
+        distances[k] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double value, int index,
+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       double* __restrict__ max_distances,
+                                       int* __restrict__ members,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                       double threshold, int n) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= n || clustered[seed]) {
+        if (seed < n && threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double* seed_max = max_distances + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    int* seed_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        seed_max[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed)
+                                  ? CUDART_INF
+                                  : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) seed_members[0] = seed;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    __shared__ double reduction_value[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int cluster_size;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        cluster_size = 1;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (cluster_size < n) {
+        double local_value = CUDART_INF;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+        const size_t distance_row = static_cast<size_t>(current_member) * n;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != CUDART_INF) {
+                value = fmax(value, distances[distance_row + candidate]);
+                seed_max[candidate] = value;
+                if (value < threshold &&
+                    betterCandidate(value, candidate, local_value, local_index)) {
+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = local_value;
+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (betterCandidate(other_value, other_index,
+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int chosen = reduction_index[0];
+            if (chosen == INT_MAX) {
+                current_member = -1;
+            } else {
+                seed_max[chosen] = CUDART_INF;
+                seed_members[cluster_size] = chosen;
+                ++cluster_size;
+                current_member = chosen;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (current_member < 0) break;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = cluster_size;
 }
 
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer_), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(pointer_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return pointer_; }
+private:
+    T* pointer_ = nullptr;
+};

+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
+        distances[k] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double value, int index,
+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       double* __restrict__ max_distances,
+                                       int* __restrict__ members,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                       double threshold, int n) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= n || clustered[seed]) {
+        if (seed < n && threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double* seed_max = max_distances + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    int* seed_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        seed_max[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed)
+                                  ? CUDART_INF
+                                  : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) seed_members[0] = seed;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    __shared__ double reduction_value[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int cluster_size;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        cluster_size = 1;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (cluster_size < n) {
+        double local_value = CUDART_INF;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+        const size_t distance_row = static_cast<size_t>(current_member) * n;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != CUDART_INF) {
+                value = fmax(value, distances[distance_row + candidate]);
+                seed_max[candidate] = value;
+                if (value < threshold &&
+                    betterCandidate(value, candidate, local_value, local_index)) {
+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = local_value;
+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (betterCandidate(other_value, other_index,
+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int chosen = reduction_index[0];
+            if (chosen == INT_MAX) {
+                current_member = -1;
+            } else {
+                seed_max[chosen] = CUDART_INF;
+                seed_members[cluster_size] = chosen;
+                ++cluster_size;
+                current_member = chosen;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (current_member < 0) break;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = cluster_size;
 }
 
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer_), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(pointer_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return pointer_; }
+private:
+    T* pointer_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+} // namespace

+namespace {
+
+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
+        distances[k] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double value, int index,
+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       double* __restrict__ max_distances,
+                                       int* __restrict__ members,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                       double threshold, int n) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= n || clustered[seed]) {
+        if (seed < n && threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double* seed_max = max_distances + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    int* seed_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        seed_max[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed)
+                                  ? CUDART_INF
+                                  : 0.0;
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) seed_members[0] = seed;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    __shared__ double reduction_value[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int cluster_size;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        cluster_size = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (cluster_size < n) {
+        double local_value = CUDART_INF;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+        const size_t distance_row = static_cast<size_t>(current_member) * n;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != CUDART_INF) {
+                value = fmax(value, distances[distance_row + candidate]);
+                seed_max[candidate] = value;
+                if (value < threshold &&
+                    betterCandidate(value, candidate, local_value, local_index)) {
+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = local_value;
+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (betterCandidate(other_value, other_index,
+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int chosen = reduction_index[0];
+            if (chosen == INT_MAX) {
+                current_member = -1;
+            } else {
+                seed_max[chosen] = CUDART_INF;
+                seed_members[cluster_size] = chosen;
+                ++cluster_size;
+                current_member = chosen;
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (current_member < 0) break;
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
+

+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       double* __restrict__ max_distances,
+                                       int* __restrict__ members,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                       double threshold, int n) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= n || clustered[seed]) {
+        if (seed < n && threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double* seed_max = max_distances + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    int* seed_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        seed_max[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed)
+                                  ? DBL_MAX
+                                  : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) seed_members[0] = seed;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    __shared__ double reduction_value[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int cluster_size;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        cluster_size = 1;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (cluster_size < n) {
+        double local_value = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+        const size_t distance_row = static_cast<size_t>(current_member) * n;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != DBL_MAX) {
+                value = fmax(value, distances[distance_row + candidate]);
+                seed_max[candidate] = value;
+                if (value < threshold &&
+                    betterCandidate(value, candidate, local_value, local_index)) {
+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = local_value;
+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (betterCandidate(other_value, other_index,
+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int chosen = reduction_index[0];
+            if (chosen == INT_MAX) {
+                current_member = -1;
+            } else {
+                seed_max[chosen] = DBL_MAX;
+                seed_members[cluster_size] = chosen;
+                ++cluster_size;
+                current_member = chosen;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (current_member < 0) break;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = cluster_size;
 }

+class DeviceBuffer {

+                  "cudaMalloc");

+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(pointer_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return pointer_; }
+private:
+    T* pointer_ = nullptr;
+};

+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;

+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
+        distances[k] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;

+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }

+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
+        distances[k] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
     }

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double value, int index,
+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);

+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,

+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != DBL_MAX) {
+                value = fmax(value, distances[distance_row + candidate]);

+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }

+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();

+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];

+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }

+

 }

+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer_), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");

+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(pointer_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return pointer_; }
+private:
+    T* pointer_ = nullptr;
+};

+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;

+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
+        distances[k] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
     }

-    return closest_point;

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double value, int index,
+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       double* __restrict__ max_distances,
+                                       int* __restrict__ members,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                       double threshold, int n) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= n || clustered[seed]) {
+        if (seed < n && threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double* seed_max = max_distances + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    int* seed_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        seed_max[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed)
+                                  ? DBL_MAX
+                                  : 0.0;
     }

+

+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int cluster_size;

+        current_member = seed;
+        cluster_size = 1;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();

+    while (cluster_size < n) {
+        double local_value = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+        const size_t distance_row = static_cast<size_t>(current_member) * n;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != DBL_MAX) {
+                value = fmax(value, distances[distance_row + candidate]);
+                seed_max[candidate] = value;
+                if (value < threshold &&
+                    betterCandidate(value, candidate, local_value, local_index)) {
+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = local_value;
+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (betterCandidate(other_value, other_index,
+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int chosen = reduction_index[0];
+            if (chosen == INT_MAX) {
+                current_member = -1;
+            } else {
+                seed_max[chosen] = DBL_MAX;
+                seed_members[cluster_size] = chosen;
+                ++cluster_size;
+                current_member = chosen;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (current_member < 0) break;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = cluster_size;
 }
 
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer_), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(pointer_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return pointer_; }
+private:
+    T* pointer_ = nullptr;
+};
+

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double value, int index,
+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       double* __restrict__ max_distances,
+                                       int* __restrict__ members,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                       double threshold, int n) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= n || clustered[seed]) {
+        if (seed < n && threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double* seed_max = max_distances + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    int* seed_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        seed_max[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed)
+                                  ? DBL_MAX
+                                  : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) seed_members[0] = seed;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    __shared__ double reduction_value[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int cluster_size;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        cluster_size = 1;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (cluster_size < n) {
+        double local_value = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+        const size_t distance_row = static_cast<size_t>(current_member) * n;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != DBL_MAX) {
+                value = fmax(value, distances[distance_row + candidate]);
+                seed_max[candidate] = value;
+                if (value < threshold &&
+                    betterCandidate(value, candidate, local_value, local_index)) {
+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = local_value;
+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (betterCandidate(other_value, other_index,
+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int chosen = reduction_index[0];
+            if (chosen == INT_MAX) {
+                current_member = -1;
+            } else {
+                seed_max[chosen] = DBL_MAX;
+                seed_members[cluster_size] = chosen;
+                ++cluster_size;
+                current_member = chosen;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (current_member < 0) break;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = cluster_size;
 }
 
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer_), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(pointer_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return pointer_; }
+private:
+    T* pointer_ = nullptr;
+};
+

+namespace {

+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;

+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
+        distances[k] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;

+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads

+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,

+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
+        distances[k] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
     }

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double value, int index,
+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       double* __restrict__ max_distances,
+                                       int* __restrict__ members,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                       double threshold, int n) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= n || clustered[seed]) {
+        if (seed < n && threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double* seed_max = max_distances + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    int* seed_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        seed_max[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed)
+                                  ? DBL_MAX
+                                  : 0.0;
     }
-

-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;

+
+    __shared__ double reduction_value[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int cluster_size;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        cluster_size = 1;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (cluster_size < n) {
+        double local_value = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+        const size_t distance_row = static_cast<size_t>(current_member) * n;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != DBL_MAX) {
+                value = fmax(value, distances[distance_row + candidate]);
+                seed_max[candidate] = value;
+                if (value < threshold &&
+                    betterCandidate(value, candidate, local_value, local_index)) {
+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = local_value;
+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (betterCandidate(other_value, other_index,
+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int chosen = reduction_index[0];
+            if (chosen == INT_MAX) {
+                current_member = -1;
+            } else {
+                seed_max[chosen] = DBL_MAX;
+                seed_members[cluster_size] = chosen;
+                ++cluster_size;
+                current_member = chosen;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (current_member < 0) break;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = cluster_size;
 }
 
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer_), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(pointer_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return pointer_; }
+private:
+    T* pointer_ = nullptr;
+};

+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
+        distances[k] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double value, int index,
+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       double* __restrict__ max_distances,
+                                       int* __restrict__ members,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                       double threshold, int n) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= n || clustered[seed]) {
+        if (seed < n && threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double* seed_max = max_distances + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    int* seed_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        seed_max[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed)
+                                  ? DBL_MAX
+                                  : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) seed_members[0] = seed;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    __shared__ double reduction_value[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int cluster_size;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        cluster_size = 1;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (cluster_size < n) {
+        double local_value = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+        const size_t distance_row = static_cast<size_t>(current_member) * n;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != DBL_MAX) {
+                value = fmax(value, distances[distance_row + candidate]);
+                seed_max[candidate] = value;
+                if (value < threshold &&
+                    betterCandidate(value, candidate, local_value, local_index)) {
+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = local_value;
+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (betterCandidate(other_value, other_index,
+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int chosen = reduction_index[0];
+            if (chosen == INT_MAX) {
+                current_member = -1;
+            } else {
+                seed_max[chosen] = DBL_MAX;
+                seed_members[cluster_size] = chosen;
+                ++cluster_size;
+                current_member = chosen;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (current_member < 0) break;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = cluster_size;
 }

-int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double value, int index,
+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       double* __restrict__ max_distances,
+                                       int* __restrict__ members,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                       double threshold, int n) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= n || clustered[seed]) {
+        if (seed < n && threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double* seed_max = max_distances + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    int* seed_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        seed_max[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed)
+                                  ? DBL_MAX
+                                  : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) seed_members[0] = seed;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    __shared__ double reduction_value[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int cluster_size;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        cluster_size = 1;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (cluster_size < n) {
+        double local_value = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+        const size_t distance_row = static_cast<size_t>(current_member) * n;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != DBL_MAX) {
+                value = fmax(value, distances[distance_row + candidate]);
+                seed_max[candidate] = value;
+                if (value < threshold &&
+                    betterCandidate(value, candidate, local_value, local_index)) {
+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = local_value;
+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (betterCandidate(other_value, other_index,
+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int chosen = reduction_index[0];
+            if (chosen == INT_MAX) {
+                current_member = -1;
+            } else {
+                seed_max[chosen] = DBL_MAX;
+                seed_members[cluster_size] = chosen;
+                ++cluster_size;
+                current_member = chosen;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (current_member < 0) break;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = cluster_size;
 }
 
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer_), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(pointer_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return pointer_; }
+private:
+    T* pointer_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+} // namespace

+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
+        distances[k] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;

+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +

+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);

+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double value, int index,
+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);

+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       double* __restrict__ max_distances,

+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != DBL_MAX) {

+                seed_max[candidate] = value;

+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = local_value;
+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (betterCandidate(other_value, other_index,
+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }

+        }
+

+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (current_member < 0) break;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = cluster_size;

+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer_), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(pointer_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return pointer_; }
+private:
+    T* pointer_ = nullptr;
+};

+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;

+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
+        distances[k] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double value, int index,
+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       double* __restrict__ max_distances,
+                                       int* __restrict__ members,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                       double threshold, int n) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= n || clustered[seed]) {
+        if (seed < n && threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double* seed_max = max_distances + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    int* seed_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        seed_max[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed)
+                                  ? DBL_MAX
+                                  : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) seed_members[0] = seed;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    __shared__ double reduction_value[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int cluster_size;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        cluster_size = 1;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (cluster_size < n) {
+        double local_value = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+        const size_t distance_row = static_cast<size_t>(current_member) * n;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != DBL_MAX) {
+                value = fmax(value, distances[distance_row + candidate]);
+                seed_max[candidate] = value;
+                if (value < threshold &&
+                    betterCandidate(value, candidate, local_value, local_index)) {
+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = local_value;
+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (betterCandidate(other_value, other_index,
+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int chosen = reduction_index[0];
+            if (chosen == INT_MAX) {
+                current_member = -1;
+            } else {
+                seed_max[chosen] = DBL_MAX;
+                seed_members[cluster_size] = chosen;
+                ++cluster_size;
+                current_member = chosen;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (current_member < 0) break;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = cluster_size;
 }

+namespace {

+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;

+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
+        distances[k] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
     }

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double value, int index,
+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       double* __restrict__ max_distances,
+                                       int* __restrict__ members,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                       double threshold, int n) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= n || clustered[seed]) {
+        if (seed < n && threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double* seed_max = max_distances + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    int* seed_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        seed_max[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed)
+                                  ? DBL_MAX
+                                  : 0.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) seed_members[0] = seed;
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    __shared__ double reduction_value[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int cluster_size;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        cluster_size = 1;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (cluster_size < n) {
+        double local_value = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+        const size_t distance_row = static_cast<size_t>(current_member) * n;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != DBL_MAX) {
+                value = fmax(value, distances[distance_row + candidate]);
+                seed_max[candidate] = value;
+                if (value < threshold &&
+                    betterCandidate(value, candidate, local_value, local_index)) {
+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = local_value;
+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (betterCandidate(other_value, other_index,
+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int chosen = reduction_index[0];
+            if (chosen == INT_MAX) {
+                current_member = -1;
+            } else {
+                seed_max[chosen] = DBL_MAX;
+                seed_members[cluster_size] = chosen;
+                ++cluster_size;
+                current_member = chosen;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (current_member < 0) break;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = cluster_size;
 }
 
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer_), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(pointer_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return pointer_; }
+private:
+    T* pointer_ = nullptr;
+};
+

+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }

+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double value, int index,
+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       double* __restrict__ max_distances,
+                                       int* __restrict__ members,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                       double threshold, int n) {

+    if (seed >= n || clustered[seed]) {

+        return;
+    }
+
+    double* seed_max = max_distances + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    int* seed_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {

     }

+        const size_t distance_row = static_cast<size_t>(current_member) * n;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != DBL_MAX) {
+                value = fmax(value, distances[distance_row + candidate]);
+                seed_max[candidate] = value;
+                if (value < threshold &&
+                    betterCandidate(value, candidate, local_value, local_index)) {
+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = local_value;
+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (betterCandidate(other_value, other_index,
+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int chosen = reduction_index[0];
+            if (chosen == INT_MAX) {
+                current_member = -1;
+            } else {
+                seed_max[chosen] = DBL_MAX;
+                seed_members[cluster_size] = chosen;
+                ++cluster_size;
+                current_member = chosen;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (current_member < 0) break;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = cluster_size;
 }
 
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer_), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(pointer_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return pointer_; }
+private:
+    T* pointer_ = nullptr;
+};

+    for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {

+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];

+        current_member = seed;
+        cluster_size = 1;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());

+        double local_value = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+        const size_t distance_row = static_cast<size_t>(current_member) * n;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != DBL_MAX) {
+                value = fmax(value, distances[distance_row + candidate]);
+                seed_max[candidate] = value;
+                if (value < threshold &&
+                    betterCandidate(value, candidate, local_value, local_index)) {
+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = local_value;
+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (betterCandidate(other_value, other_index,
+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int chosen = reduction_index[0];
+            if (chosen == INT_MAX) {
+                current_member = -1;
+            } else {
+                seed_max[chosen] = DBL_MAX;
+                seed_members[cluster_size] = chosen;
+                ++cluster_size;
+                current_member = chosen;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (current_member < 0) break;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = cluster_size;
 }

+private:
+    T* pointer_ = nullptr;

+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
+        distances[k] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double value, int index,
+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);
+}

+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,

+

+namespace {

+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;

+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}

+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
+        distances[k] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
     }

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool betterCandidate(double value, int index,
+                                                 double best_value, int best_index) {
+    return value < best_value || (value == best_value && index < best_index);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one seed's complete greedy candidate cluster. Threads
+// cooperate across candidate points; all seed clusters execute concurrently.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const double* __restrict__ distances,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       double* __restrict__ max_distances,
+                                       int* __restrict__ members,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities,
+                                       double threshold, int n) {
+    const int seed = blockIdx.x;
+    if (seed >= n || clustered[seed]) {
+        if (seed < n && threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    double* seed_max = max_distances + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    int* seed_members = members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * n;
+    for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+        seed_max[candidate] = (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed)
+                                  ? DBL_MAX
+                                  : 0.0;
     }

-        *cluster_members = members;

+    __syncthreads();
+
+    __shared__ double reduction_value[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int reduction_index[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int current_member;
+    __shared__ int cluster_size;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        current_member = seed;
+        cluster_size = 1;
     }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (cluster_size < n) {
+        double local_value = DBL_MAX;
+        int local_index = INT_MAX;
+        const size_t distance_row = static_cast<size_t>(current_member) * n;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            double value = seed_max[candidate];
+            if (value != DBL_MAX) {
+                value = fmax(value, distances[distance_row + candidate]);
+                seed_max[candidate] = value;
+                if (value < threshold &&
+                    betterCandidate(value, candidate, local_value, local_index)) {
+                    local_value = value;
+                    local_index = candidate;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = local_value;
+        reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_value = reduction_value[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = reduction_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (betterCandidate(other_value, other_index,
+                                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x],
+                                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    reduction_value[threadIdx.x] = other_value;
+                    reduction_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int chosen = reduction_index[0];
+            if (chosen == INT_MAX) {
+                current_member = -1;
+            } else {
+                seed_max[chosen] = DBL_MAX;
+                seed_members[cluster_size] = chosen;
+                ++cluster_size;
+                current_member = chosen;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (current_member < 0) break;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = cluster_size;
 }
 
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&pointer_), count * sizeof(T)),
+                  "cudaMalloc");
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { cudaFree(pointer_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* get() const { return pointer_; }
+private:
+    T* pointer_ = nullptr;
+};

+constexpr int CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE = 256;
+
+void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " +
+                                 cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Distances are reused by every seed and every outer QT iteration.  Computing
+// this matrix once removes the sqrt from the much hotter greedy search kernel.
+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, int n) {
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(n) * n;
+    for (size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         k < total; k += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
+        const int i = static_cast<int>(k / n);
+        const int j = static_cast<int>(k - static_cast<size_t>(i) * n);
+        const double dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
+        const double dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
+        distances[k] = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
